Given f normalized sum = sqrt(2n) or sum=1. Algorithm:
1. f nonnegative, length n, sum s_target = sqrt(2n) maybe. M=||f*f||∞.
2. Solve LP: maximize sum g_j s.t. g>=0, ||f*g||∞ <= M. Use only constraints for full convolution length 2n-1? They denote f*g0 maybe full. Extreme point determined by active binding constraints. Instead of solving LP exactly, could use an off-the-shelf LP solver with sparse matrix.
3. Rescale g to sum s_target. Blend f <- (1-t)f + t g. Repeat.

Why this doesn't get stuck at uniform? For uniform f, M average? LP may have multiple optima; if solve symmetric, g uniform. But if include tie-breaking or random perturbations, maybe finds non-uniform extreme. Need break symmetry. Could start from nonuniform initialization. Could use LP with small random perturbation on objective (max sum g + ε·rand·g) to select nontrivial extreme. Then blend.

Can we solve LP faster using dual and active set? Since f positive, A_f is banded Toeplitz-like. We can implement using scipy.linprog? Let's evaluate. n=3809, constraints m=2n-1=7617, vars n. Implementation details:
```
def softmax(theta):
    th = theta - theta.mean()
    m = th - th.max()
    p = np.exp(m)
    p /= p.sum()
    return p, m? 
```

Simplex projection function:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    r = np.arange(1, v.size+1)
    cond = u - (cssv - 1)/r > 0
    if not cond.any():
       # all negative, uniform?
    idx = np.where(cond)[0][-1]
    theta = (cssv[idx] -1)/(idx+1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
Assumes v sum maybe not1; projection onto sum=1. If updates small and v positive, can use faster: `q = v - v.min(); s=q.sum(); if s>0 q/=s else uniform`. But projection exact maybe.

Let's design robust code structure:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def fast_conv(p):
   L=1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(2*n-1)))
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)[:2*n-1]
   c[(c<0)&(c<1e-15)]=0
   return c

def fast_grad_for_weights(p, w):
   # w length 2n-1 sum maybe1
   # compute h_j = sum_k w_k p_{k-j}
   Lg=1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(3*n-2)))
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,Lg)
   wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],Lg)
   r=np.fft.irfft(fp*wr,Lg)
   h = r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   return h
```
Check `irfft` length Lg returns length Lg real; indices fine. But computing fp each time. For c and grad in same iteration, we can compute fp with L=16384, c from first 7617; grad uses same fp with w_rev. Need L=16384 enough for both conv c length 7617 (L>=7617) and grad length 3n-2=11426 (L>=11426). So use L=16384 for all. `fp=nrfft(p,L)`. c = ifft(fp*fp)[:2n-1]. For grad, `wr=nrfft(w[::-1], L)`; `r=irfft(fp*wr, L)`; h = r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]. Good. One rfft p, one irfft c, one rfft w, one irfft grad. We can precompute w_rev with reversal.
Potential FFT error: coefficients might have tiny negative; cap.

Soft objective:
```
def objective(p, beta):
    c=fast_conv(p)
    cmax=c.max()
    if cmax <=0: return 0, cmax, None
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # clip z > -50
    z[z<-50]= -50
    ex=np.exp(z)
    w=ex/ex.sum()
    soft=cmax + math.log(ex.sum())/beta  # if we use beta? For gradient formula weights only; soft value not needed exact.
    return soft, cmax, w
```
If we include log term, gradient of soft still weighted average (because derivative of log term cancels). So weights as above. Use soft for line search? We can just use cmax exact (max) as score surrogate; but gradient uses weights. For line search, use exact score maybe from cmax. Simpler: accept if exact max decreases. Use weights to guide.

We can use subgradient with active set: choose c within `active_tol` of max. This avoids beta parameter. `w = (c >= cmax - tol)/num_active`. Tol maybe `1e-7` absolute? c~2e-4; to focus near max, tol=1e-8 or relative 1%. If too narrow, gradient noisy. Use beta=1e5 as smooth.

Compute gradient g_p = 2h (since derivatives). Use weights sum1. Descent direction. If objective max scale, gradient scale. Fast convolution for p sum1:
```
def fft_power_L(p): # p length n, nonneg
    L = 1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()  # for p^2
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, L)[:2*n-1]
    # numerical tiny negatives
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0) # But if negative significant due error, okay; score might lower? For surrogate only.
    return c
```
For gradient using same L maybe need Lg=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length(). To avoid recomputing, use Lg for both; c = ifft(fp*fp)[:2n-1] valid though Lg larger. Use same L = pow2 >=3n-2 (16384) for all. `fp` computed with L. For c, convolution with zero padding to L; first 2n-1 correct. Good.
```
L = 1 << max(1, (3*n-2).bit_length())
```

Low-dimensional spline details:
Knots: 13 or 11. For p generation:
```
def seq_from_q_s(q, s):
    i = np.arange(n)
    x = i/ (n-1)
    qq = np.interp(x, knot_x, q)  # q length K
    den = n - i + s  # s >0; last n-(n-1)+s =1+s
    raw = qq / np.sqrt(den)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 0)
    sraw=raw.sum()
    if sraw <=0: return uniform
    p = raw / sraw
    return p
```
Alternatively `den=(n-i)/n + 1e-6`? The distance scale affects continuous singular. Use den = (n - i) maybe if s=1: last1, first n? If den=n-i+s with s=0, last=1; if s=1, last=2, first n+1. - best_q = np.ones(K), best_s=0.2?, best_score=large.

```
T = 1e-3? Score differences ~0.01. Set T=1e-4 initially, cool to1e-6.
for it in range(max_iters):
  if time > deadline_low: break
  sigma_q=0.15*cool +0.01
  sigma_s=0.1
  q_new = np.clip(best_q * np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma_q,K)), 1e-3, 10)
  s_new = np.clip(best_s + rng.normal(0,sigma_s), 0.0, 5.0)
  if rng.random()<0.5: perturb one knot more.
  score_new=fast_score_seq(q_new,s_new)
  if score_new < best_score or random < exp(-(score_new-best_score)/T): best=...
  if score_new < global_best_score: update global

Stage 2 low-dim code details:
```
def q_candidate_score(q, s):
    # q, s
    x = np.arange(n)/ (n-1)
    qq = np.interp(x, knots, q) # ensure no NaN
    raw = qq / np.sqrt(n - np.arange(n) + s)
    raw = np.clip(raw, 0, None)
    ssum=raw.sum()
    if ssum <= 1e-15: return inf, uniform_p
    p=raw/ssum
    c=fast_conv(p)
    score=2*n*max(0,c.max())
    return score,p
```

Let's specify full loop pseudo:
```
current = best_p.copy()
cur_score = best_score_fast
T = 1e-5
noimprove=0
while time<full_deadline:
  r = rng.random()
  # every 1000 iters, if noimprove>500, snapshot current=best, T=add heat
  if r < 0.2:
     # gradient move using current or best
     base = best_p if noimprove>200 else current
     score, cmax, h = score_and_grad(base, beta)
     # choose gamma backtracking
     gamma = 0.02
     improved_move=False
     for _ in range(4):
        cand = base - gamma*h
        if cand.min()<0: cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
        cand = normalize(cand)
        sc = fast_score(cand)
        if sc < score or (T>1e-8 and rng.random()<np.exp(-(sc-score)/T)):
            current=cand; cur_score=sc; improved_move=True; break
        gamma*=0.5
  elif r<0.55:
     cand = block_multiply(current, rng)
     sc=fast_score(cand)

  elif r<0.8:

def _fast_conv_max(p, L):
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, L)
    c = c[:len(p)*2-1]
    # remove small neg
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < 0: cmax=0
    score = 2.0*p.size*cmax / (p.sum()**2) # if p not normalized; but we normalize, could omit sum
    return score, c, fp
```

Random block perturb:
```
def _block_perturb(p, rng):
   n=p.size
   # choose width lognormal around 50
   width = int(math.exp(rng.normal(math.log(min(max(n//100,5), n//10)), 0.8)))
   width = int(np.clip(width, 1, max(2,n//3)))
   start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
   sigma = float(np.exp(rng.normal(math.log(0.15),0.6))) # 0.03-1
   factor = math.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma))
   candidate = p.copy()
   candidate[start:start+width] *= factor
   # occasionally add linear trend in block
   if rng.random()<0.2:
      trend = np.linspace(0.5+rng.normal(0,0.1), 1.5+rng.normal(0,0.1), width)
      candidate[start:start+width] *= trend.max()? 
   return _normalize_positive(candidate)
```
Maybe random lognormal 0.15 too small; full search may need larger. Use sigma lognormal mean0.2. For low-dim q not needed.

def _gradient_perturb(p, L, beta, gamma):
   score,cmax,fp,w = _score_and_weights(p,L,beta)
   # if cmax==0 return p
   wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L); r=np.fft.irfft(fp*wr,L); h=r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
   h=np.maximum(h,0); hs=h.sum()
   if hs>0: h/=hs
   else: h=np.full(n,1/n)
   candidate = p - gamma*h
   candidate = np.maximum(candidate,0); renorm
   return candidate
```

Detailed code outline:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s = 1300.0
    deadline = start + max(1.0, budget_s - 2.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # parent
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and np.isfinite(par).all():
            parent_arr = np.clip(par, 0.0, None)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None
    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
        n = int(parent_arr.size)
    else:
        n = 3809
    if n <= 0: n=3809
    if n > 50000: n = 3809 # maybe cap? But if parent bigger? Don't use that? But no.
    if n == 1: return [1.0]

    m = 2*n -1
    L = 1 << max(1, (3*n-2).bit_length())
    xs_full = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n-1)
    den_base = (n - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64))  # n..1
    uniform_p = np.full(n, 1.0/n, dtype=np.float64)
```
If n capped to3809 ignoring parent length, but using parent score maybe length different? If parent length >50000, returning n3809 maybe fine. We'll not cap? Maybe keep original if 10<=n<=50000. If too large, use 3809.

`fast_score`:
```
    def fast_score_p(p):
        s = p.sum()
        if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return math.inf
        p = p / s
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, L)[:m]
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax < 0 or not math.isfinite(cmax): return math.inf
        return 2.0 * n * cmax
```
Here p normalized inside. For repeated p already normalized; still OK but sum cost O(n). Could skip if p_sum1. Use.

`fast_score_and_grad`:
```
    def grad_direction(p, beta=50000.0):
        p = _norm(p)
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=...; cmax=...
        z=beta*(c-cmax); z = np.where(z < -70, -70, z)
        w=np.exp(z); w = np.maximum(w,0); wsum=w.sum()
        if wsum <=0: w=np.ones(m)/m
        else: w/=wsum
        wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L)
        r=np.fft.irfft(fp*wr,L)
        h = r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1].copy()
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h,0)
        hs = h.sum()
        if hs <=0: h = uniform_p.copy()
        else: h /= hs
        score = 2.0*n*cmax
        return score, h
```
If p normalized, score. If cmax from c with tiny negatives, okay.

`normalize_positive`:
```
    def make_pos(v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = v.sum()
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return uniform_p.copy()
        return v / s
```
This enforces sum1. For values all zero, uniform.

`build_q`:
```
    K = min(13, max(5, n//200?)) but fixed 11. If n small, K maybe.
    K = 11 if n>=20 else 5
    knots_x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,K)
    q = np.ones(K)
    s_param=0.0
    def build_q_p(qv, sp):
        # qv positive
        qv = np.maximum(qv, 1e-6)
        qq = np.interp(xs_full, knots_x, qv)
        den = den_base + max(0.0, sp)
        raw = qq / np.sqrt(den)
        return make_pos(raw)
```
If n=2, xs [0,1], OK.

    def score_q(qv, sp):

        return fast_score_p(p), p
```

Exact helper:

    def exact_for_p(p):
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
        if arr.size ==0: return math.inf
        # scale max to 1 maybe but p sum1 fine. For safety if max>1000: arr = arr/arr.max()*0.5
        if arr.max() > 1000.0:
            arr = arr / arr.max() * 0.5
        if arr.sum() < 0.01:
            arr = arr + 1.0
        return float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
```

Block move candidate:
```
def block_candidate(p):
   c=p.copy()
   # width distribution: use lognormal mean sqrt(n/20)? n=3809 mean13? Need effective. We want blocks ~1-1000. Use width = int(np.exp(rng.normal(math.log(20),1.0))) clipped 1..n//2.
   width = int(math.exp(rng.normal(math.log(max(5, n//50)), 1.0)))
   width= int(min(max(width,1), n//2))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
   sigma = 0.05 + 0.6*max(T/1e-4,1e-6)**0.5
   factor=math.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma))
   c[start:start+width] *= factor
   if rng.random()<0.25:
      # add slope
      slope = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)
      lin = 1 + slope * np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,width)
      lin = np.maximum(lin, 0.1)
      c[start:start+width] *= lin
   return make_pos(c)
```
If factor huge, p clipped and normalize. Good.

factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
return make_pos(p*factor)

Now, code for coordinate_q. Let's define before main loop after preset:
```
    def coordinate_q(q_in, s_in, max_time):
       q=np.array(q_in,dtype=float).copy(); s=float(s_in); sc,p=score_q(q,s)
       t0=time.monotonic(); factors=(0.7,0.85,1.15,1.3,0.5,1.5)
       while time.monotonic()<t0+max_time:
          improved=False
          for i in range(K):
             for f in factors:
                q2=q.copy(); q2[i]=min(10.0,max(1e-3,q[i]*f))
                sc2,_=score_q(q2,s)
                if sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
                   q=q2; sc=sc2; improved=True
          if s>1e-6:
             for f in (0.7,0.9,1.1,1.3):
                s2=max(0.0,s*f); sc2,_=score_q(q,s2)
                if sc2 < sc -1e-11: s=s2; sc=sc2; improved=True
          else:
             for s2 in (0.25,0.5,1.0):
                sc2,_=score_q(q,s2)
                if sc2 < sc-1e-11: s=s2; sc=sc2; improved=True
          if not improved: break
       return q,s,sc
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    """
    Search nonnegative step heights minimizing the exact AC1 evaluator.

    The method uses:
      - a length-3809/parent length by default,
      - fast FFT surrogates for the squared convolution,
      - a parametric family with a reflected Euler-violated tail and smooth
        amplitude corrections,
      - low-dimensional annealing/coordinate improvement,
      - higher-dimensional local perturbations for polishing,
      - exact evaluation preservation for the final returned candidate.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    if not math.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s <= 2.0:
        budget_s = 2.0

    # Leave headroom for final exact validation and return.
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 3.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Parent/length
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    parent_arr = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and np.isfinite(par).all():
            parent_arr = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.size > 0:
        n = int(parent_arr.size)
        if n < 10:
            n = 3809
        # The intended construction is 3809; do not use an unexpectedly
        # huge parent vector, because dense/temporary costs would explode.
        if n > 60000:
            n = 3809
    else:
        n = 3809

    if n < 2:
        try:
            evaluate_sequence([1.0])
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    m = 2 * n - 1
    # One working Fourier size sufficient for both p*p and gradient convolutions.
    L = 1 << max(1, (3 * n - 2).bit_length())

    xs_full = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n - 1))
    den_base = n - np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)  # n, n-1, ..., 1
    uniform_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Basic helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def make_pos(v):
        """Clip to nonnegative and rescale to sum 1 (safe for the exact score)."""
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
        if v.size != n:
            v = np.resize(v, n)
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        s = v.sum()
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return uniform_p.copy()
        return v / s

    def fast_score_p(p):
        """Fast surrogate exact-objective value (FFT)."""
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        s = p.sum()
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return math.inf
        p = p / s
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)[:m]
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
            return math.inf
        return 2.0 * float(n) * cmax

    def score_and_direction(p, beta=60000.0):
        """
        Returns (fast_score, normalized direction h) where p - gamma*h is a
        subgradient-style descent direction for the max convolution peak.
        """
        p = make_pos(p)
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)[:m]
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            return math.inf, uniform_p.copy()

        z = beta * (c - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < -70.0, -70.0, z)
        w = np.exp(z)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
        ws = w.sum()
        if not math.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.fill(m, 1.0 / float(m))
        else:
            w = w / ws

        wr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
        r = np.fft.irfft(fp * wr, L)
        h = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1].copy()
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        hs = h.sum()
        if not math.isfinite(hs) or hs <= 0.0:
            h = uniform_p.copy()
        else:
            h = h / hs

        return 2.0 * float(n) * cmax, h

    def exact_for_p(p):
        """Authoritative evaluator on a normalized p array."""
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = arr.sum()
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return math.inf
        arr = arr / s
        if arr.size == 0:
            return math.inf
        # Values are already <= 1 after normalization, no clipping issue.
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
        except Exception:
            val = math.inf
        try:
            val = float(val)
        except Exception:
            val = math.inf
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return math.inf
        return val

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Parametric family:
    # raw_i = q(i/n) * (n - i + tau)^(-beta)
    # q is a positive interpolation spline; beta near 1/2 is the natural
    # finite-length endpoint tail; tau smooths the first endpoint.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if n >= 80:
        K = 13
    elif n >= 30:
        K = 9
    else:
        K = 5

    knots_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K, dtype=np.float64)

    def build_q_p(qv, tau, beta):
        qv = np.asarray(qv, dtype=np.float64)
        if qv.size != K:
            qv = np.resize(qv, K)
        qv = np.where(np.isfinite(qv), qv, 1.0)
        qv = np.maximum(qv, 1e-4)
        qq = np.interp(xs_full, knots_x, qv)
        tau = float(max(0.0, tau))
        den = den_base + tau
        beta = float(beta)
        if not math.isfinite(beta):
            beta = 0.5
        beta = float(np.clip(beta, 0.0, 0.75))
        if beta < 1e-8:
            raw = qq
        else:
            raw = qq * np.power(den, -beta)
        return make_pos(raw)

    def score_q(qv, tau, beta):
        p = build_q_p(qv, tau, beta)
        sc = fast_score_p(p)
        return sc, p

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial exact fallback and best trackers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Uniform always has exact score 2.0, except for harmless numerical ties.
    best_exact_score = 2.0
    best_exact_list = [1.0] * n

    if parent_arr is not None:
        try:
            par_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(parent_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)]
            par_val = float(evaluate_sequence(par_list))
            if math.isfinite(par_val) and par_val < best_exact_score:
                best_exact_score = par_val
                best_exact_list = par_list
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_p = None
    best_fast = math.inf
    last_exact = t0

    def consider_p(p):
        nonlocal best_p, best_fast
        if p is None:
            return
        sc = fast_score_p(p)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_fast:
            best_fast = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

    def try_exact_if_useful():
        nonlocal best_exact_score, best_exact_list, last_exact
        if best_p is None:
            return
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 6.0:
            return
        if best_fast >= best_exact_score - 1e-9:
            return
        if time.monotonic() - last_exact < 0.8:
            return
        try:
            val = exact_for_p(best_p)
        except Exception:
            return
        last_exact = time.monotonic()
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = val
            best_exact_list = [float(x) for x in best_p]

    # Parent as one start point.
    if parent_arr is not None and parent_arr.sum() > 0:
        consider_p(parent_arr)

    # Uniform fallback start.
    consider_p(uniform_p)

    # Initial parametric presets.
    q_state = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    tau_state = 0.25
    beta_state = 0.5
    q_state_score = math.inf

    preset_qs = []
    ones = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    preset_qs.append(ones)

    xk = knots_x
    for a in (0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45):
        preset_qs.append(1.0 - a * (np.sin(np.pi * xk) ** 2))
        preset_qs.append(
            1.0
            - 0.5 * a * np.exp(-np.square((xk - 0.42) / 0.18))
            - 0.5 * a * np.exp(-np.square((xk - 0.58) / 0.18))
        )
    for a in (0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.28):
        preset_qs.append(1.0 - a * xk * (1.0 - xk))
        preset_qs.append(1.0 - 0.5 * a * xk)
        preset_qs.append(1.0 - 0.5 * a * (1.0 - xk))
    preset_qs.append(1.0 + 0.15 * xk - 0.35 * xk * (1.0 - xk))
    preset_qs.append(1.0 - 0.05 + 0.10 * np.exp(-np.square((xk - 0.25) / 0.20)))

    # A few random positive smooth initial shapes.
    for _ in range(18):
        b1 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, K)
        b2 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, K)
        qrand = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(np.pi * xk) * b1 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * xk) * b2
        qrand = np.exp(np.clip(qrand - 1.0, -2.0, 2.0))
        preset_qs.append(qrand)

    if budget_s > 120.0:
        tau_list = (0.0, 0.25, 0.75, 2.0, 5.0)
        beta_list = (0.35, 0.45, 0.50, 0.55, 0.60)
    else:
        tau_list = (0.0, 1.0)
        beta_list = (0.45, 0.50, 0.55)

    t_pres = time.monotonic()
    for qv in preset_qs:
        qv = np.clip(np.asarray(qv, dtype=np.float64), 1e-3, 10.0)
        med = float(np.median(qv))
        if med > 0.0:
            qv = qv / med
        for tau in tau_list:
            for beta in beta_list:
                sc, p = score_q(qv, tau, beta)
                if not math.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < best_fast:
                    best_fast = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()
                if sc < q_state_score:
                    q_state_score = sc
                    q_state = qv.copy()
                    tau_state = float(tau)
                    beta_state = float(beta)
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break

    # Normalize current best and q state.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = uniform_p.copy()
        best_fast = 2.0
    current_p = best_p.copy()
    cur_score = float(best_fast)

    # Ensure q_state corresponds to a feasible candidate.
    if not math.isfinite(q_state_score):
        q_state_score = best_fast
        q_state = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        tau_state = 0.25
        beta_state = 0.5
        q_state = q_state / float(np.median(q_state))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Coordinate improvement for the low-dimensional q/tau/beta state
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def coordinate_q(q_in, tau_in, beta_in, max_time):
        t_c0 = time.monotonic()
        max_time = max(0.5, float(max_time))
        q = np.clip(np.asarray(q_in, dtype=np.float64), 1e-3, 10.0).copy()
        tau = float(max(0.0, tau_in))
        beta = float(np.clip(beta_in, 0.0, 0.75))
        sc, p = score_q(q, tau, beta)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            return q, tau, beta, sc, p

        q_factors = (0.70, 0.85, 1.15, 1.30, 0.50, 1.50)
        tau_factors = (0.60, 0.80, 1.25, 1.60, 0.0)
        beta_adds = (-0.02, -0.01, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05)

        while time.monotonic() < t_c0 + max_time:
            improved = False

            for i in range(K):
                for f in q_factors:
                    q2 = q.copy()
                    q2[i] = float(np.clip(q[i] * f, 1e-3, 10.0))
                    med = float(np.median(q2))
                    if med > 0.0:
                        q2 = q2 / med
                    sc2, p2 = score_q(q2, tau, beta)
                    if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
                        q, tau, beta, sc, p = q2, tau, beta, sc2, p2
                        improved = True

            if tau > 1e-6:
                for f in tau_factors:
                    tau2 = float(np.clip(tau * f, 0.0, 20.0)) if f > 0 else 0.0
                    sc2, p2 = score_q(q, tau2, beta)
                    if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
                        q, tau, beta, sc, p = q, tau2, beta, sc2, p2
                        improved = True
            else:
                for tau2 in (0.25, 0.75, 1.5, 3.0):
                    sc2, p2 = score_q(q, tau2, beta)
                    if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
                        q, tau, beta, sc, p = q, tau2, beta, sc2, p2
                        improved = True

            for db in beta_adds:
                beta2 = float(np.clip(beta + db, 0.0, 0.75))
                sc2, p2 = score_q(q, tau, beta2)
                if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-11:
                    q, tau, beta, sc, p = q, tau, beta2, sc2, p2
                    improved = True

            if not improved:
                break

        return q, tau, beta, sc, p

    # Run a bounded coordinate phase if there is enough time.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 30.0:
        coord_budget = min(60.0, max(5.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35))
        q_state, tau_state, beta_state, q_state_score, p_state = coordinate_q(
            q_state, tau_state, beta_state, coord_budget
        )
        if math.isfinite(q_state_score) and q_state_score < best_fast:
            best_fast = q_state_score
            best_p = p_state.copy()
            current_p = best_p.copy()
            cur_score = best_fast
            try_exact_if_useful()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Local high-dimensional moves
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def block_candidate(p):
        c = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        upper = max(2, n // 3)
        mean_w = max(5.0, min(200.0, n / 30.0))
        width = int(math.exp(rng.normal(math.log(mean_w), 1.0)))
        width = int(np.clip(width, 1, upper))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        stop = min(n, start + width)

        sigma = float(np.clip(rng.normal(math.log(0.18), 0.6), 0.03, 1.2))
        c[start:stop] *= math.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))

        if rng.random() < 0.35:
            slope = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)
            lin = 1.0 + slope * np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, stop - start)
            lin = np.maximum(lin, 0.05)
            c[start:stop] *= lin

        return make_pos(c)

    def cosine_candidate(p):
        c = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        amp = float(np.clip(rng.normal(0.10, 0.05), 0.03, 0.25))
        factor = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * xs_full + phase)
        factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.05)
        return make_pos(c * factor)

    def gradient_candidate(p):
        old_score, h = score_and_direction(p, beta=60000.0)
        if not math.isfinite(old_score):
            return p.copy(), old_score
        base_gamma = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.003, 0.006, 0.012], dtype=float)))
        best_cand = p.copy()
        best_sc = old_score
        for gamma in (base_gamma, 0.5 * base_gamma, 0.25 * base_gamma):
            cand = make_pos(p - gamma * h)
            sc = fast_score_p(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_cand = cand
            if math.isfinite(sc) and (sc < old_score - 1e-10 or sc < best_fast - 1e-10):
                break
        return best_cand, best_sc

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main annealing search
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    T = 1.0e-4
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    def accept_temperature(diff_score, temperature):
        if not math.isfinite(diff_score):
            return False
        if diff_score <= 0.0:
            return True
        if temperature <= 1e-12:
            return False
        if diff_score > temperature * 25.0:
            return False
        try:
            return bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-diff_score / temperature))
        except Exception:
            return False

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        it += 1
        T = max(1e-9, 1e-4 * math.exp(-float(it) / 50000.0))
        sigma = max(0.02, 0.25 * math.sqrt(max(T, 1e-12) / 1e-4))

        # If stuck, re-center current state and optionally re-coord.
        if no_improve > 2500:
            current_p = best_p.copy()
            cur_score = float(best_fast)
            T = max(T, 1.0e-5)
            no_improve = 0
            if deadline - time.monotonic() > 25.0 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                q_state, tau_state, beta_state, q_state_score, p_state = coordinate_q(
                    q_state, tau_state, beta_state, min(8.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
                )
                if math.isfinite(q_state_score) and q_state_score < best_fast:
                    best_fast = q_state_score
                    best_p = p_state.copy()
                    current_p = best_p.copy()
                    cur_score = best_fast
                    try_exact_if_useful()

        move = rng.random()

        if move < 0.52:
            # Low-dimensional q/tau/beta move.
            qnew = q_state * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K))
            qnew = np.clip(qnew, 1e-3, 10.0)
            med = float(np.median(qnew))
            if med > 0.0:
                qnew = qnew / med

            if rng.random() < 0.45:
                idx = int(rng.integers(0, K))
                qnew[idx] = float(np.clip(qnew[idx] * math.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35)), 1e-3, 10.0))

            tau_new = float(tau_state + (0.25 * sigma * T / 1e-4) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                tau_new = float(np.clip(tau_new, 0.0, 20.0))
            else:
                tau_new = float(np.clip(tau_state, 0.0, 20.0))

            beta_new = float(np.clip(beta_state + 0.05 * sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0), 0.0, 0.75))

            sc, pnew = score_q(qnew, tau_new, beta_new)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                no_improve += 1
                continue

            if sc < q_state_score - 1e-11 or accept_temperature(sc - q_state_score, T):
                q_state = qnew
                tau_state = tau_new
                beta_state = beta_new
                q_state_score = sc
                if sc < cur_score:
                    current_p = pnew.copy()
                    cur_score = sc

            if sc < best_fast:
                best_fast = sc
                best_p = pnew.copy()
                current_p = pnew.copy()
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                try_exact_if_useful()
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        elif move < 0.77:
            cand = block_candidate(current_p)
            sc = fast_score_p(cand)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            if sc < best_fast:
                best_fast = sc
                best_p = cand.copy()
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                try_exact_if_useful()
            elif sc < cur_score - 1e-11 or accept_temperature(sc - cur_score, T):
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        elif move < 0.90:
            cand = cosine_candidate(current_p)
            sc = fast_score_p(cand)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            if sc < best_fast:
                best_fast = sc
                best_p = cand.copy()
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                try_exact_if_useful()
            elif sc < cur_score - 1e-11 or accept_temperature(sc - cur_score, T):
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        else:
            cand, sc = gradient_candidate(current_p)
            if cand is None or sc is None:
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            sc = float(fast_score_p(cand))
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                no_improve += 1
                continue
            if sc < best_fast:
                best_fast = sc
                best_p = cand.copy()
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                try_exact_if_useful()
            elif sc < cur_score - 1e-11 or accept_temperature(sc - cur_score, T):
                current_p = cand.copy()
                cur_score = sc
            else:
                no_improve += 1

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact validation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    candidates = []
    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(best_p)
    if current_p is not None and not np.allclose(current_p, best_p, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-15):
        candidates.append(current_p)
    candidates.append(uniform_p)

    last_exact = t0  # final checks may bypass rate limit
    for p in candidates:
        if p is None:
            continue
        try:
            val = exact_for_p(p)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = val
            best_exact_list = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)]

    # Guarantee a valid finite list.
    try:
        if len(best_exact_list) == 0:
            best_exact_list = [1.0] * n
        if any((not math.isfinite(x)) or x < 0.0 for x in best_exact_list):
            best_exact_list = [1.0] * n
    except Exception:
        best_exact_list = [1.0] * n

    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
        pass  # Already at/over internal deadline; harness budget enforced by execution manager.

    return best_exact_list
```
